# Optional execution overrides (merged into strategy in compare_historical.py)
# passive + limit_offset_cents: 0 → quote mid (spread squeeze); 1 → bid + 1¢
execution:
  price_strategy: aggressive # passive | aggressive | market — cross spread when edge clears (PnL / fills on fast crypto books)
  # Penny-jump: bid+1¢ (or min with ask−1¢) for queue priority vs stink bids
  limit_offset_cents: 2 # Increased from 1 to jump more aggressively
  # PnL: if passive/mid, force dip-style limits when time-to-resolution ≤ this many hours (inclusive at boundary)
  near_expiry_aggressive_hours: 2.0 # Increased from 1.0
  # Cancel resting BUY when live bid has risen this far above our limit (frees capital; reprices next cycle).
  resting_cancel_if_market_bid_above_limit_by: 0.01 # Tightened from 0.02 to re-quote faster
  # Sell held side when net edge (model − bid − maker fee) falls below this floor; 0 = off.
  # Negative value = require clear reversal before exiting (hysteresis buffer).
  position_exit_min_net_edge: 0.01 # Exit as soon as edge is gone (was -0.02)
  # Minimum minutes to hold a position before discretionary exits (edge-exit, likely-loser, flip).
  # Stop-loss and rule-violation still fire immediately. 0 = off.
  position_min_hold_minutes: 5 # Reduced from 10
  # Conviction-based hold: if entry edge was >= this, use the stronger (more negative) exit floor below.
  position_exit_strong_entry_edge: 0.20 # Increased from 0.15
  position_exit_strong_entry_floor: -0.02 # Tightened (was -0.04)
  # After this many minutes, if held net edge is still below position_exit_stale_edge_floor, exit (slow bleed / bag-hold).
  # Not blocked by min-hold. 0 = off.
  position_exit_stale_minutes: 30 # Reduced from 60
  # Stale exit when held_edge < this (default 0 = any negative edge after stale_minutes).
  position_exit_stale_edge_floor: 0.02 # Tightened (was 0.0)
  # When true: large net edge → quote just inside ask; medium → bid+tick; else dip/layered path.
  limit_buy_edge_aggression_enabled: true
  # EV-tier pricing (PnL / fills): strong → at ask; medium → bid + fraction×spread or mid; weak → bid; else skip.
  limit_buy_ev_tier_pricing: true
  limit_buy_weak_bid_min_edge: 0.04 # Slightly lower (was 0.05)
  limit_buy_skip_below_weak_edge: true
  limit_buy_medium_quote_mode: spread_fraction # spread_fraction | mid
  limit_buy_medium_spread_fraction: 0.95 # More aggressive (was 0.90)
  # Tier thresholds (cross ≥ near ≥ weak). Align with recovery/strategy min_edge where possible.
  limit_buy_cross_spread_min_edge: 0.10 # Lower threshold to take ask (was 0.12)
  limit_buy_near_market_min_edge: 0.05 # (was 0.07)
  # Below this net edge, skip EV cross/medium tiers (maker-style dip/layered only). 0 = off.
  limit_buy_passive_only_below_edge: 0.03 # (was 0.05)
  # Resting BUY: nudge limit +1¢ at each minute threshold (requires open order age from exchange).
  limit_buy_escalation_price_enabled: true
  limit_buy_escalate_after_minutes_1: 2 # Faster (was 5)
  limit_buy_escalate_after_minutes_2: 5 # Faster (was 10)
  # Optional: cancel stale resting BUYs earlier than limit_order_ttl (0 = off). 15 = patience exhausted.
  limit_buy_escalation_cancel_minutes: 10 # Added 10min cap (was 0)
  # Avoid stacking another same-side BUY while an unresolved position exists for that market (platform+ticker+side).
  skip_new_buy_when_unresolved_same_side: true
  # Wide/gapped order books: lift dip-based limits toward bid + anchor×(ask−bid), capped by model fair value;
  # EV-tier quotes also get a touch-lift to at least the bid when fair_cap allows.
  limit_buy_wide_book_coalesce_enabled: true
  limit_buy_wide_book_spread_rel: 0.15 # Tightened (was 0.22)
  limit_buy_wide_book_anchor_fraction: 0.80 # Increased (was 0.70)
  limit_buy_wide_book_conservative_substrings: []
  limit_buy_wide_book_conservative_yf_tickers: []

# Optional recovery overlay (merged into strategy before execution merge). Use for low-balance “get gears turning”.
# recovery.min_edge overrides strategy.min_edge (legacy path). With simple_ev on, simple_ev_decision.min_edge is the base gate.
recovery:
  # Merged into strategy.min_edge — keep aligned with simple_ev / strategy min_edge
  min_edge: 0.05 # Tightened from 0.03
  adaptive_edge_max_extra: 0.0 # 0 = no recent-WR bump while in recovery; when lifting recovery, strategy uses capped extras above
  loss_streak_max_extra: 0.0 # 0 = no streak bump while in recovery; pair with adaptive_learning_enabled when you re-enable
  force_min_buy_contracts_one: true # After budget pass, promote best-edge zero-count BUY to x1 if it fits max_total_buy_pct slice
  # Without these, low min_edge is negated: calibration adds +edge when WR<50%; parity adds up to ~20% on the dominant side.
  calibration_extra_edge_overall: 0.02 # Small penalty for bad history (was 0)
  # Looser than 0.07: allow momentum-style “price already above strike” YES without choking fills
  calibration_extra_edge_above_yes: 0.05 # Tightened (was 0.03)
  parity_penalty_enabled: true # Re-enabled to avoid one-sidedness (was false)
  # Empirical CI + vol-disagree: re-enabled so required edge scales with data confidence and thin disagreements can trade.
  uncertainty_edge_mult: 0.20 # Tightened (was 0.10)
  vol_disagree_extra_edge: 0.03 # Tightened (was 0.01)
  yes_overconfidence_extra_edge: 0.01 # Added small bias correction (was 0)
  # Turn off multipliers that were inflating required edge ~1.08× + DTE scaling on top of CI.
  enable_vol_regime_scaling: true # Re-enabled for risk scaling
  dte_edge_scaling_enabled: true # Re-enabled to prioritize short DTE
  # >1.0 stacks on simple_ev and legacy min_edge for 15m / sub-hour (simple_ev path applies this in compare_historical)
  ultra_short_min_edge_mult: 1.2 # Increased (was 1.0)
  # Hourly / non–5pm crypto (and non–4pm S&P) use strict mode: model P(win) must exceed these AND edge — funding wallets does not change that.
  intraday_other_min_p_win: 0.65 # Tightened (was 0.60)
  intraday_other_min_p_win_with_vol: 0.60 # Tightened (was 0.55)
  # 0 = off. A low strategy cap (e.g. 22¢) drops almost every BUY (69¢ YES / 31¢ NO both fail).
  max_buy_limit_price: 0

# Strategy for compare_historical.py (BUY YES / BUY NO / SKIP)
strategy:
  emergency_stop_reset_time: 1776032100
  # vol_regime | vol_regime_threshold (aliases): VolRegimeStrategy = DefaultThreshold recommend + vol mults in compare_historical
  strategy_engine: vol_regime
  # When simple_ev_decision.enabled is true: single net-edge gate + Kelly sizing; barbell/parity/vol-disagree do not stack on min_edge.
  simple_ev_decision:
    enabled: true
    # Net edge after maker fee; 15% base for higher hurdle (was 5%)
    min_edge: 0.15
    # No BUY when model P(win) on the bought side is in this band (ambiguous “coin flip” zone).
    skip_p_win_mid_band_enabled: true # Re-enabled to avoid coin flips
    skip_p_win_mid_lo: 0.42
    skip_p_win_mid_hi: 0.58
    # New proposed fields:
    no_trade_zone_width: 0.10
    buy_no_extra_min_edge: 0.07
    # 0–10¢ legs must clear this net edge (blocks weak edge at penny prices; 0 = off).

    cheap_band_price_lo: 0.00
    cheap_band_price_hi: 0.10
    cheap_band_min_net_edge: 0.12 # Tightened (was 0.06)
    kelly_dampen: 0.10 # Reduced to decrease size (was 0.20)
    max_bankroll_fraction: 0.05 # Increased from 0.01 to allow larger trades
    # Strong-edge sizing: when net edge clears this, multiply spend fraction (still bounded by max_bankroll_fraction).
    edge_size_boost_edge: 0.15 # Tightened (was 0.10)
    edge_size_boost_mult: 1.2 # Reduced (was 1.5)
    spread_wide_fraction: 0.05 # Tightened (was 0.10)
    spread_wide_size_mult: 0.3 # Tightened (was 0.5)
    # Multiply simple-EV spend fraction on 15m / sub-hour (1.0 = unchanged). Compensates for same Kelly path on fast markets.
    ultra_short_size_mult: 0.5 # Reduced (was 0.8)
    emp_vol_disagree_threshold: 0.10 # Tightened (was 0.15)
    disagree_size_mult: 0.3 # Tightened (was 0.5)
    calibration_win_rate_threshold: 0.45 # Tightened (was 0.40)
    calibration_poor_size_mult: 0.5 # Tightened (was 0.7)
    min_bid_size: 0 # Hard skip when visible bid size below this; 0 = use min_order_book_depth only
  # Dynamic edge by price band: require higher edge for 'coin flip' zones (40-60c) or weak bands.
  tiered_min_edge:
    enabled: true
    min_edge: 0.15 # Tightened from 0.05 to focus on high-signal trades
    bands:
      - { lo: 0.00, hi: 0.02, min_edge: 0.25 } # Ultra-cheap: require edge (was 20¢)
      - { lo: 0.02, hi: 0.15, min_edge: 0.20 } # Cheap tails
      - { lo: 0.15, hi: 0.40, min_edge: 0.15 } # Standard 'sweet spot'
      - { lo: 0.40, hi: 0.65, min_edge: 0.20 } # Coin-flip zone
      - { lo: 0.65, hi: 1.00, min_edge: 0.15 } # Expensive consensus
  # Log final required edges: simple mode prints the single gate; legacy prints stacked core + req_yes/req_no.
  log_effective_min_edge: true
  # One JSON line per market with p pipeline, net edges, required hurdles, regime mults (grep decision_diag:).
  log_decision_diagnostics: true # ENABLED for live calibration test: captures P-shifts for analysis
  # When true: drop pending BUYs whose win range does not overlap an already-kept bet (same yf+resolution+platform).
  # Default false — true blocked many valid multi-strike edges (e.g. INXU hourly ladder) for disjoint ranges.
  filter_disjoint_underlying_buys: false
  # Base min_edge (overridden by recovery.min_edge when set). Net edge after fees: model P − market − fee must clear this.
  # Used when simple_ev_decision.enabled is false. With simple EV on, simple_ev_decision.min_edge is the active gate.
  min_edge: 0.15 # Tightened from 0.04
  # Barbell: only "base" (high-P consensus) or "alpha" (cheap + large net edge after fees); middle-priced edges skipped.
  # Off while simple_ev_decision is primary — avoids subtle double-routing; re-enable after baseline is stable.
  barbell_enabled: true # Re-enabled for conservative consensus (was false)

  enable_easy_win: true
